**Draw** (`drawParticles`, 717-726): `canvas.drawCircle(px, py, 5f, particlePaint)`, `particlePaint.alpha = plife*255`. One fixed color (`0,230,118`), one fixed radius, one shape.

**What it cannot currently do:** no per-particle color, size, rotation, or sprite; no drag, no wind, no attractor; no additive/glow blend mode; no trails; no burst intensity parameter beyond `count`; no lifetime-driven size curve; no spawn on anything except a correct note.

## 11. Per-frame budget

`doFrame` (420-515) runs at `Choreographer` cadence; `frameIntervalNanos` recomputed from `display?.refreshRate` every frame (default `8_333_333L` = 120Hz → **8.33 ms budget**). Gate per `CLAUDE.md`/SPEC §1: zero dropped frames at 120Hz with 200 live particles; `droppedFrames++` when `delta > frameIntervalNanos * 3 / 2` (line 436), i.e. >12.5 ms. `WARMUP_FRAMES = 12` excluded.

**Per-frame CPU work:** refresh query, frame stats + EMA, wait-mode clamp, `pumpAutoplay` (autoplay only), `ring.drain`, `judge.advanceTo`, `updateParticles`, HUD rebuild **only when the judged total changes**, `perfLine` rebuild once per second, `RemoteLog` summary every 5 s when enabled.

**Per-frame draw calls:**
| Pass | Calls |
|---|---|
| `drawColor(BLACK)` | 1 |
| `drawNotes` | ≤ notes inside `LOOKAHEAD_MS = 3000f`; 1 `drawRoundRect` each + 1 `drawText` when `bottom-top > 44f` |
| `drawParticles` | `particleCount` × `drawCircle` (0–512; gate target 200) |
| `drawKeyboard` | 88 `drawRect` (52 white + 36 black, `lowNote=21`…`highNote=108`) + 52 `drawText` + 52 `measureText` + 1 `drawCircle` (middle C). Recall mode: 88 rects + 1 circle only |
| hit line | 1 `drawLine` |
| HUD **or** progress | 2 `drawText`, **or** `notes.size` `drawRect` (8 for a standard drill) |
| prompt / end text | ≤ 2 `drawText` |

≈ **150–210 draw ops per frame** in the normal case, ~350 with a saturated particle pool.

**Allocation audit of the hot path — one real violation:** `markLandmarkGroup` (line 655) does `intArrayOf(1, 3)` / `intArrayOf(6, 8, 10)` **inside `drawNotes`, every frame**, whenever a revealed recall prompt is on screen. That is a per-frame heap allocation in the render loop, which `CLAUDE.md` classes as a bug. Everything else is clean: `keyDue` cleared with `Arrays.fill`, all `Paint`s and `StringBuilder`s pre-allocated, `noteLabels` pre-built (lines 165-167), `canvas.drawText(CharSequence, start, end, …)` overload used for the StringBuilders. Minor: `System.nanoTime()` is called inside `whiteKeyPaintFor`/`blackKeyPaintFor`, i.e. **88 times per frame** (lines 663, 674).

## 12. Places a reward moment could attach but currently does not

**In the highway (`NoteHighwayView`):**
- **Combo milestones** — `judge.combo`/`bestCombo` are live at line 502 and drive nothing; no burst scale, color shift, or hit-line intensity tied to streak.
- **Timing quality per hit** — `bestAbs` and `errMs[best]` are computed in `HitJudge.onNoteOn` (86-101) and discarded until the end-of-run average; no "perfect/early/late" per-note signal, no burst variation by precision.
- **Wrong note** — only the 450 ms red key. No burst, no sound, no error-type hint (the octave/neighbour classification in `FoundationsTrainer.classifyError` is post-hoc DB analysis only).
- **Force-miss / timeout** (line 468-477) — silent.
- **Reveal fires** (line 460-465) — the answer appears with no transition; the moment the learner "gets rescued" is unmarked.
- **The final note of a run** — `ended = true` at line 486 fires `rebuildEndText` + `onEnded` with no burst, no flourish, no hold. In both hosts the view is destroyed immediately, so `endLine1`/`endLine2` are dead code outside free play.
- **Progress bar completion** — `drawDrillProgress` reaching full is not distinguished from any other segment fill.
- **Beat pulse** — already a per-beat clock signal, unused as a reward channel.
- **`hitNotePaint` white flash** — currently the only per-hit chart-side signal, no fade, no scale.

**In foundations (`FoundationsScreen` / `FoundationsTrainer`):**
- `daysCredited++` (`FoundationsTrainer.kt:360`) — the 3-days-to-mastery counter increments invisibly.
- `mastered()` flipping true (line 104) — no event; only the `"✓ "` prefix and `Mint2` color on next recomposition.
- `st.rung++` (line 382) — the visible difficulty ladder advancing shows up only as `"+1 oct"` appended to a row.
- `songGateOpen()` becoming true (`FoundationsScreen.kt:112`) — the gate to the entire song side opens with a silent `prefs.edit()`; the button label merely changes from `"Songs anyway"` to `"Go to song"`.
- `sittingFinishLabel()` (line 417) — the named end-of-sitting state is a plain green sentence.
- `summary` (line 103) — the drill verdict has no visual container, no comparison to the previous drill, no delta.

**In sessions (`SessionEngine` / `SessionScreen`):**
- `Phase.VICTORY` (line 300-312) — the engine explicitly constructs a guaranteed-success final rep, and the UI renders it as an ordinary briefing.
- `recordFinishCandidate` (line 335) firing at `accuracyPercent >= CLEAN_AT (85f)` — a clean pass is captured into a label but produces nothing at the moment it happens.
- Ladder promotion at `cleanStreak >= 2` (lines 165-169) — moving up a rung is invisible; the learner only sees the tempo/hand text change in the next `stepLabel`.
- `Diagnosis.of` returning `"Clean. …"` (line 363) — the clean case renders in the same gray `0xFF78828C` as the failure diagnostics.
- `FinishedContent` — static text; no session-level summary of reps, no comparison with prior sessions.

**Global:** no audio channel is available for reward at all during live play (`DemoToneSynth` is architecturally reserved for listen/demo per SPEC §1), and no haptic channel is wired up anywhere in the codebase.
